12763:   async function drawMeshMorphFrame(canvas, t, options={}){
12764:     const photos=(window.cfGetMorphSource?window.cfGetMorphSource():state.photos)||[];
12765:     const aIndex = Number.isInteger(options.aIndex) ? options.aIndex : (state.morphPair?.a ?? 0);
12766:     const bIndex = Number.isInteger(options.bIndex) ? options.bIndex : (state.morphPair?.b ?? Math.min(1,photos.length-1));
12767:     const a=photos[aIndex], b=photos[bIndex];
12768:     const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
12769:     const w=canvas.width, h=canvas.height;
12770:     ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0);
12771:     ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
12772:     drawCaseflowGradientBg(ctx,w,h);
12773:     if(!a || !b){
12774:       ctx.fillStyle='rgba(32,27,53,.62)';
12775:       ctx.font='800 30px -apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,sans-serif';
12776:       ctx.fillText('Select at least 2 images',44,72);
12777:       return {triangles:0,points:0,userCount:0,fallback:true};
12778:     }
12779:     const e=ease(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t)));
12780:     // Read mode config — all behaviour is driven from cfMorphVariantDefs so future
12781:     // expansions only require changing the config object, not this render loop.
12782:     const _vdef=cfGetVariantDef(window.cfMorphVariant);
12783:     const _dentalBlendEnabled=_vdef.dentalBlend||!!window.CASEFLOW_DEBUG_DENTAL_BLEND;
12784:     // ── Per-pair geometry cache (preview stutter fix) ──────────────────────────
12785:     // Images, base canvases, dental ROI, pair risk (getImageData-based!) and morph
12786:     // pairs are stable for a given (pair, canvas size, face mesh, variant); they
12787:     // were being recomputed on EVERY preview frame, which is what made playback
12788:     // stutter. Only the t-dependent work (mid lerp + Delaunay + warp) stays
12789:     // per-frame. Face-mesh arrival auto-invalidates via the _faceMesh reference;
12790:     // cfCapRefresh clears the cache after alignment/landmark edits.
12791:     let GC=window.__cfMorphGeoCache;
12792:     if(!(GC && GC.a===a && GC.b===b && GC.w===w && GC.h===h &&
12793:          GC.fmA===a._faceMesh && GC.fmB===b._faceMesh &&
12794:          GC.variant===(window.cfMorphVariant||'clinical'))){
12795:       const [imgA,imgB]=await Promise.all([loadImage(a.dataUrl),loadImage(b.dataUrl)]);
12796:       const rA=fitContain(imgA.naturalWidth||imgA.width,imgA.naturalHeight||imgA.height,w,h,0);
12797:       const rB=fitContain(imgB.naturalWidth||imgB.width,imgB.naturalHeight||imgB.height,w,h,0);
12798:       const bA=makeBaseCanvas(imgA,w,h,rA);
12799:       const bB=makeBaseCanvas(imgB,w,h,rB);
12800:       const _preRoi=cfComputeDentalROI(a,b,rA,rB,w,h);
12801:       var _pairRisk={level:'low',score:0,coarseWeight:0.20,factors:{}};
12802:       try{
12803:         _pairRisk=cfAssessPairRisk(a,b,bA,bB,_preRoi);
12804:         if(window.CASEFLOW_DEBUG) console.log('[CW] variant:',window.cfMorphVariant||'clinical','risk:',_pairRisk.level,'score:',_pairRisk.score.toFixed(3),'cw:',_pairRisk.coarseWeight.toFixed(2));
12805:       }catch(e2){}
12806:       // coarseWeight + mode bias → oral exclusion zone expansion (1.1=tight, 1.6=wide)
12807:       const _effectiveCW=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,_pairRisk.coarseWeight+(_vdef.coarseWeightBias||0)));
12808:       const _oralExpand=1.1+_effectiveCW*0.5;
12809:       const collected=collectMorphPairs(a,b,rA,rB,w,h,_oralExpand,bA,bB);
12810:       GC=window.__cfMorphGeoCache={
12811:         a:a, b:b, w:w, h:h, fmA:a._faceMesh, fmB:b._faceMesh,
12812:         variant:(window.cfMorphVariant||'clinical'),
12813:         rectA:rA, rectB:rB, baseA:bA, baseB:bB,
12814:         pairs:dedupePairs(collected.pairs), oralROI:collected.oralROI,
12815:         userCount:collected.userCount
12816:       };
12817:     }
12818:     const rectA=GC.rectA, rectB=GC.rectB, baseA=GC.baseA, baseB=GC.baseB;
12819:     const pairs=GC.pairs;
12820:     const oralROI=GC.oralROI;
12821:     // Endpoint-preserving morph: eW=e means t=0→image A exactly, t=1→image B exactly.
12822:     // coarseWeight shapes mesh topology only (oral exclusion zone), never eW itself.
12823:     const eW=e;
12824:     const mid=pairs.map(p=>({x:p.p1.x*(1-eW)+p.p2.x*eW,y:p.p1.y*(1-eW)+p.p2.y*eW}));
12825:     const p1=pairs.map(p=>p.p1), p2=pairs.map(p=>p.p2);
12826:     const tris=delaunay(mid);
12827:     if(!state.trueMeshEnabled || tris.length<1){
12828:       // Cross-dissolve: A opaque base, B fades in — no white midpoint.
12829:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=1; ctx.drawImage(baseA,0,0); ctx.restore();
12830:       ctx.save(); ctx.globalAlpha=e; ctx.drawImage(baseB,0,0); ctx.restore();
12831:     }else{
12832:       // A as the opaque warped base, B warped over it.
12833:       tris.forEach(tr=>{
12834:         const [i,j,k]=tr;
12835:         drawWarpedTriangle(ctx,baseA,p1[i],p1[j],p1[k],mid[i],mid[j],mid[k],1);
12836:       });
12837:       // ── Seamless texture handoff ───────────────────────────────────────────
12838:       // Beier–Neely morph: A and B are BOTH warped to the same intermediate
12839:       // shape, then cross-dissolved. To avoid a "static → sudden reveal →
12840:       // static" feel (a mid-clip texture burst reads as a seam), the dissolve
12841:       // is driven by the EASED geometry position eW — so shape and texture
12842:       // advance together across the whole clip. A wide window [0.12,0.88] keeps
12843:       // a slight shape-lead while guaranteeing pristine endpoints (t=0 → pure A,
12844:       // t=1 → pure B). window.CF_MORPH_TEXTURE_MODE='legacy' restores alpha=e;
12845:       // window.CF_TEX_WINDOW=[a,b] tunes the window (now measured on eW).
12846:       const _texMode=window.CF_MORPH_TEXTURE_MODE||'warp_dominant';
12847:       const _tw=(Array.isArray(window.CF_TEX_WINDOW)&&window.CF_TEX_WINDOW.length===2)?window.CF_TEX_WINDOW:[0.12,0.88];
12848:       // If the pair has no real geometry (no face mesh / no user landmarks →
12849:       // boundary-only pairs, max displacement ~0), tie the blend straight to e.
12850:       if(GC.maxDisp===undefined){
12851:         let _md=0;
12852:         for(const p of pairs){ const dx=p.p2.x-p.p1.x, dy=p.p2.y-p.p1.y; const d=dx*dx+dy*dy; if(d>_md) _md=d; }
12853:         GC.maxDisp=Math.sqrt(_md);
12854:       }
12855:       const _texA=(_texMode==='legacy'||GC.maxDisp<2) ? e
12856:         : (function(x){ x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,(x-_tw[0])/Math.max(0.05,_tw[1]-_tw[0]))); return x*x*(3-2*x); })(eW);
12857:       // Skip the whole B layer while its alpha is 0 (t below the window) —
12858:       // roughly halves per-frame triangle draws early in each segment.
12859:       if(_texA>0.001) tris.forEach(tr=>{
12860:         const [i,j,k]=tr;
12861:         drawWarpedTriangle(ctx,baseB,p2[i],p2[j],p2[k],mid[i],mid[j],mid[k],_texA);
12862:       });
12863:       // ── Dental ROI alpha blend ─────────────────────────────────────────────
12864:       // Controlled by variant config (dentalBlend) or debug override.
12865:       // Currently false in all 3 modes — the composite caused patch artifacts
12866:       // around the lower lip and chin. Re-enable per mode via cfMorphVariantDefs
12867:       // once a patch-free implementation is developed.
12868:       if(_dentalBlendEnabled){
12869:         const _droi=cfComputeDentalROI(a,b,rectA,rectB,w,h);

28738:   function ss(x){ x=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,x)); return x*x*(3-2*x); }
28739:   function toastFn(m){ if(typeof toast==='function') toast(m); else console.log(m); }
28740: 
28741:   /* ---- phase remap: case type → warp progress + local mask ops ---- */
28742:   function planPhases(t, plan){
28743:     var ops=[]; var warpT=t;
28744:     var mk=plan.masks||{};
28745:     var appl=[].concat(mk.brackets||[], mk.wires||[], mk.attachments||[]);
28746:     var emrg=[].concat(mk.missing_spaces||[], mk.prosthetic_emergence_regions||[]);
28747:     var extr=[].concat(mk.extraction_spaces||[]);
28748:     switch(plan.case_type){
28749:       case 'appliance_cleanup':
28750:         warpT=ss((t-0.20)/0.80);
28809:   /* ---- wrap the shared production renderer (preview AND export resolve
28810:      window.cfDrawMorphFrame at call time, so both get the same event plan) ---- */
28811:   function installWrap(){
28812:     if(window.__cfEventWrapInstalled || typeof window.cfDrawMorphFrame!=='function') return;
28813:     window.__cfEventWrapInstalled=true;
28814:     var core=window.cfDrawMorphFrame;
28815:     window.cfDrawMorphFrame=async function(canvas, t, options){
28816:       var plan=state.morphEventPlan;
28817:       /* Rollback switch (§legacy fallback): window.CF_DISABLE_EVENT_MORPH=true
28818:          restores the previous stable renderer without removing the feature.
28819:          t always comes from the caller — the phase remap derives from it and
28820:          still progresses 0→1; it is never frozen to a constant. */
28821:       if(window.CF_DISABLE_EVENT_MORPH || !plan || !plan.confirmed) return core(canvas, t, options);
28822:       var ph=planPhases(Math.max(0,Math.min(1,t)), plan);
28823:       var out=await core(canvas, ph.warpT, options);
28824:       try{
28825:         var photos=(typeof window.cfGetMorphSource==='function')?window.cfGetMorphSource():((state&&state.photos)||[]);
28826:         var aIdx=(options&&options.aIndex)||0;
28827:         applyMaskOps(canvas, photos[aIdx], ph.ops);
28828:       }catch(e){ /* mask pass must never break the frame */ }
28829:       return out;
28830:     };
28831:   }
28832:   installWrap();
28833:   document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', installWrap);
28834:   setTimeout(installWrap, 1500);
28835:   /* Export path (renderExportFrame) applies the same plan via these hooks. */
28836:   window.cfEventPlanPhases=planPhases;
28837:   window.cfEventApplyMaskOps=function(canvas, beforePhoto, ops){ applyMaskOps(canvas, beforePhoto, ops); };
28838:   /* Geometry-cache invalidation: alignment/landmark edits re-enter via
28839:      cfCapRefresh — clear the per-pair cache so new landmarks take effect. */
28840:   (function wrapRefresh(){
28841:     var tries=0;
28842:     var iv2=setInterval(function(){
28843:       var pr=window.cfCapRefresh;
28844:       if(typeof pr==='function' && !pr.__cfGeoWrap){
28845:         var wr=function(){ try{ window.__cfMorphGeoCache=null; }catch(e){} return pr.apply(this,arguments); };
28846:         wr.__cfGeoWrap=true; window.cfCapRefresh=wr; clearInterval(iv2);
28847:       } else if(++tries>30) clearInterval(iv2);
28848:     },400);
28849:   })();
28850: 
28851:   /* ---- AI analysis call (server-side Gemini; key never in frontend) ---- */
28852:   function downscale(dataUrl, maxDim){
28853:     return new Promise(function(resolve){
28854:       var im=new Image();
28855:       im.onload=function(){
28856:         var s=Math.min(1, maxDim/Math.max(im.naturalWidth, im.naturalHeight));
28857:         var c=document.createElement('canvas');
28858:         c.width=Math.max(2,Math.round(im.naturalWidth*s)); c.height=Math.max(2,Math.round(im.naturalHeight*s));
28859:         c.getContext('2d').drawImage(im,0,0,c.width,c.height);
28860:         try{ resolve(c.toDataURL('image/jpeg',0.85)); }catch(e){ resolve(dataUrl); }
28861:       };
28862:       im.onerror=function(){ resolve(dataUrl); };
28863:       im.src=dataUrl;
28864:     });
28865:   }
28866:   async function analyzeCase(){
28867:     var photos=(typeof window.cfGetMorphSource==='function')?window.cfGetMorphSource():((state&&state.photos)||[]);
28868:     if(!photos || photos.length<2){ toastFn('BeforeとAfterの2枚が必要です'); return; }
28869:     var sb=window.cfSupabase;
28870:     var jwt=null;
28871:     try{ var s=await sb.auth.getSession(); jwt=s&&s.data&&s.data.session&&s.data.session.access_token; }catch(e){}
28872:     if(!jwt){ toastFn('ログインが必要です'); return; }
28873:     toastFn('AIが症例構造を解析中…');
28874:     var before=await downscale(photos[0].dataUrl, 900);
28875:     var after=await downscale(photos[photos.length-1].dataUrl, 900);
28876:     try{
28877:       var res=await fetch('/api/dental_event_plan',{
28878:         method:'POST',
28879:         headers:{ 'Content-Type':'application/json','Authorization':'Bearer '+jwt },
28880:         body:JSON.stringify({ before:before, after:after })
28881:       });
28882:       var j=await res.json();
28883:       if(res.ok && j && j.ok){ showConfirmSheet(j); }
28884:       else if(j && j.reason==='gemini_not_configured'){ showConfirmSheet(null, 'AI解析は未設定です（サーバーにGEMINI_API_KEYが必要）。症例タイプを手動で選択できます。'); }
28885:       else { showConfirmSheet(null, 'AI解析に失敗しました。症例タイプを手動で選択できます。'); }
28886:     }catch(e){ showConfirmSheet(null, '通信エラー。症例タイプを手動で選択できます。'); }
28887:   }

28891:   function esc(s){ return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(c){return {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c];}); }
28892:   function showConfirmSheet(ai, note){
28893:     if(!sheetOv){
28894:       sheetOv=document.createElement('div');
28895:       sheetOv.id='cfEventSheet';

28897:       document.body.appendChild(sheetOv);
28898:       sheetOv.addEventListener('click',function(e){ if(e.target===sheetOv) sheetOv.style.display='none'; });
28899:     }
28900:     var sel=(ai&&ai.case_type)||'unknown';
28901:     var evHtml=(ai&&ai.detected_events&&ai.detected_events.length)
28902:       ? ai.detected_events.map(function(ev){
28903:           return '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;padding:7px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(124,92,255,.10);font-size:11.5px">'+

12440:   async function cfEnsureFaceMesh(photo){

12460:   // oralExpand: how far to extend the oral exclusion zone around face-mesh points
12461:   // (1.1 = tight, 1.6 = very wide). Driven by cfAssessPairRisk coarseWeight.
12462:   // ── Grid block-matching correspondences (Focus/intraoral) ──────────────────
12463:   // Two composited base canvases → sparse grid of TRUE point correspondences via
12464:   // coarse-to-fine NCC block matching. Unlike outline heuristics these vectors
12465:   // track real pixel structures, so warp motion follows actual tooth/gingiva
12466:   // features instead of pseudo-landmarks. Low-confidence and outlier vectors are
12467:   // rejected; on failure returns [] and the caller falls back to prior behaviour.
12468:   // window.CF_DISABLE_FLOW_LM=true disables (rollback).
12469:   function cfFlowGridPairs(baseA, baseB, w, h){
12470:     function gray(cv, gw){
12471:       const gh=Math.max(24,Math.round(gw*h/w));
12472:       const c=document.createElement('canvas'); c.width=gw; c.height=gh;
12473:       const x=c.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});
12474:       x.drawImage(cv,0,0,gw,gh);
12475:       const d=x.getImageData(0,0,gw,gh).data;
12476:       const g=new Float32Array(gw*gh);
12477:       for(let i=0;i<gw*gh;i++) g[i]=d[i*4]*0.299+d[i*4+1]*0.587+d[i*4+2]*0.114;
12478:       return {g:g,w:gw,h:gh};
12479:     }
12480:     function ncc(A,B,ax,ay,bx,by,r){
12481:       let sa=0,sb=0,saa=0,sbb=0,sab=0,n=0;
12482:       for(let dy=-r;dy<=r;dy++)for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++){
12483:         const va=A.g[(ay+dy)*A.w+ax+dx], vb=B.g[(by+dy)*B.w+bx+dx];
12484:         sa+=va; sb+=vb; saa+=va*va; sbb+=vb*vb; sab+=va*vb; n++;
12485:       }
12486:       const ma=sa/n, mb=sb/n;
12487:       const va_=saa/n-ma*ma, vb_=sbb/n-mb*mb;
12488:       if(va_<18||vb_<18) return -2;                       // 無地ブロックは信頼不可
12489:       return (sab/n-ma*mb)/Math.sqrt(va_*vb_);
12490:     }
12491:     function bestMatch(A,B,ax,ay,cx,cy,search,r){
12492:       let bs=-2,bx=cx,by=cy;
12493:       const x0=Math.max(r,cx-search), x1=Math.min(B.w-1-r,cx+search);
12494:       const y0=Math.max(r,cy-search), y1=Math.min(B.h-1-r,cy+search);
12495:       for(let yy=y0;yy<=y1;yy++)for(let xx=x0;xx<=x1;xx++){
12496:         const s=ncc(A,B,ax,ay,xx,yy,r);
12497:         if(s>bs){ bs=s; bx=xx; by=yy; }
12498:       }
12499:       return {s:bs,x:bx,y:by};
12500:     }
12501:     const A1=gray(baseA,132), B1=gray(baseB,132);
12502:     const A2=gray(baseA,264), B2=gray(baseB,264);
12503:     const step=20, m=17, cand=[];
12504:     for(let gy=m;gy<A2.h-m;gy+=step)for(let gx=m;gx<A2.w-m;gx+=step){
12505:       // 粗探索(1/2解像度) → 精密化(基準解像度)
12506:       const c1=bestMatch(A1,B1,gx>>1,gy>>1,gx>>1,gy>>1,14,4);
12507:       if(c1.s<0.25) continue;
12508:       const c2=bestMatch(A2,B2,gx,gy,Math.min(A2.w-9,Math.max(8,c1.x*2)),Math.min(A2.h-9,Math.max(8,c1.y*2)),4,6);
12509:       if(c2.s<0.45) continue;
12510:       const vx=c2.x-gx, vy=c2.y-gy;
12511:       if(Math.hypot(vx,vy)>A2.w*0.17) continue;           // 非現実的な大変位は除外
12512:       cand.push({x:gx,y:gy,vx:vx,vy:vy,s:c2.s});
12513:     }
12514:     // 近傍中央値との乖離が大きい外れベクトルを除去（局所平滑性の担保）
12515:     function med(arr){ const a2=arr.slice().sort((p,q)=>p-q); return a2[a2.length>>1]; }
12516:     const keep=[];
12517:     for(const p of cand){
12518:       const nb=cand.filter(q=>Math.abs(q.x-p.x)<=step*2 && Math.abs(q.y-p.y)<=step*2);
12519:       if(nb.length<3) continue;
12428:     return c;
12429:   }

12433:     pairs.push({p1,p2,type});

12481:       let sa=0,sb=0,saa=0,sbb=0,sab=0,n=0;
12482:       for(let dy=-r;dy<=r;dy++)for(let dx=-r;dx<=r;dx++){

12485:       }

12489:       return (sab/n-ma*mb)/Math.sqrt(va_*vb_);
12490:     }
12491:     function bestMatch(A,B,ax,ay,cx,cy,search,r){
12492:       let bs=-2,bx=cx,by=cy;
12493:       const x0=Math.max(r,cx-search), x1=Math.min(B.w-1-r,cx+search);
12494:       const y0=Math.max(r,cy-search), y1=Math.min(B.h-1-r,cy+search);
12495:       for(let yy=y0;yy<=y1;yy++)for(let xx=x0;xx<=x1;xx++){
12496:         const s=ncc(A,B,ax,ay,xx,yy,r);
12497:         if(s>bs){ bs=s; bx=xx; by=yy; }
12498:       }
12499:       return {s:bs,x:bx,y:by};
12500:     }

12502:     const A2=gray(baseA,264), B2=gray(baseB,264);
12503:     const step=20, m=17, cand=[];
12504:     for(let gy=m;gy<A2.h-m;gy+=step)for(let gx=m;gx<A2.w-m;gx+=step){
12505:       // 粗探索(1/2解像度) → 精密化(基準解像度)

12514:     // 近傍中央値との乖離が大きい外れベクトルを除去（局所平滑性の担保）
12515:     function med(arr){ const a2=arr.slice().sort((p,q)=>p-q); return a2[a2.length>>1]; }
12516:     const keep=[];
12517:     for(const p of cand){

12528:     return out;

20980: /* ---------- Swipe-from-left = back gesture (smartphone navigation) ---------- */
20981: (function(){
20982:   if(window.__cfSwipeBack) return; window.__cfSwipeBack=true;
20983: 
20984:   // Lightweight navigation history so a back-swipe returns to the previous screen.
20985:   var navHistory=[], navigatingBack=false;
20986:   var prevGo=window.go;
20987:   window.go=function(id){
20988:     var cur=document.querySelector('.screen.active');
20989:     var curId=cur && cur.id;
20990:     var r = prevGo ? prevGo.apply(this,arguments) : undefined;
20991:     if(!navigatingBack && curId && curId!==id){
20992:       if(navHistory[navHistory.length-1]!==curId){
20993:         navHistory.push(curId);
20994:         if(navHistory.length>60) navHistory.shift();
20995:       }
20996:     }
20997:     return r;
20998:   };
20999:   window.cfGoBack=function(){
21000:     if(!navHistory.length) return false;
21001:     var target=navHistory.pop();
21002:     navigatingBack=true;
21003:     try{ window.go(target); } finally { navigatingBack=false; }
21004:     return true;
21005:   };
21006: 
21007:   // If an overlay / modal / sheet is open, let its own close control handle dismissal
21008:   // instead of navigating the screen underneath.
21009:   function overlayOpen(){
21010:     var sel='.cropModal,.photoManagerModal,.sampleVideoOverlay,.sampleVideoSheet,.studioChooserOverlay,.studioChooser,[role="dialog"]';
21011:     var els=document.querySelectorAll(sel);
21012:     for(var i=0;i<els.length;i++){
21013:       var el=els[i];
21014:       if(el.offsetParent===null) continue;            // not rendered
21015:       var cs=getComputedStyle(el);
21016:       if(cs.display!=='none' && cs.visibility!=='hidden' && parseFloat(cs.opacity||'1')>0.02 && el.offsetWidth>0 && el.offsetHeight>0){
21017:         return true;
21018:       }
21019:     }
21020:     return false;
21021:   }
21022: 
21023:   // Edge-swipe (start near the left edge, drag right) — mirrors the iOS back gesture and
21024:   // avoids clashing with before/after compare sliders or horizontal content drags.
21025:   var sx=0, sy=0, st=0, tracking=false;
21026:   var EDGE=32, DIST_X=64, MAX_Y=80, MAX_T=800;
21027:   document.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
21028:     if(e.touches.length!==1){ tracking=false; return; }
21029:     var t=e.touches[0];
21030:     if(t.clientX<=EDGE && !overlayOpen()){ sx=t.clientX; sy=t.clientY; st=Date.now(); tracking=true; }
21031:     else tracking=false;
21032:   },{passive:true});
21033:   document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
21034:     if(!tracking) return;
21035:     var t=e.touches[0]; if(!t) return;
21036:     // cancel if the gesture turns mostly vertical (it's a scroll, not a back-swipe)
21037:     if(Math.abs(t.clientY-sy) > Math.abs(t.clientX-sx) && Math.abs(t.clientY-sy)>24) tracking=false;
21038:   },{passive:true});
21039:   document.addEventListener('touchend',function(e){
21040:     if(!tracking) return; tracking=false;
21041:     var t=e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]; if(!t) return;
21042:     var dx=t.clientX-sx, dy=t.clientY-sy, dt=Date.now()-st;
21043:     if(dx>=DIST_X && Math.abs(dy)<=MAX_Y && dt<=MAX_T && !overlayOpen()){
21044:       window.cfGoBack();
21045:     }
21046:   },{passive:true});
21047: })();

14098: <script>
14099: /* ---------- v7.5 Landmark Morph Studio: gradient-backed preview/export + watermark ---------- */
14100: (function(){
14101:   const $id=id=>document.getElementById(id);
14102:   const NS='http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
14103:   const WATERMARK_SRC = 'media/inline/watermark.png';
14104:   window.state = window.state || {};
14105:   state.v75ActiveIndex = Number.isInteger(state.v75ActiveIndex) ? state.v75ActiveIndex : (Number.isInteger(state.v74ActiveIndex) ? state.v74ActiveIndex : 0);

14107:   state.v75Settings = Object.assign({warp:100, flips:0, duration:3.0, size:'9:16'}, state.v74Settings || state.v75Settings || {});
14108: 
14109:   function toastFn(msg){ if(typeof toast==='function') toast(msg); else console.log(msg); }
14110:   function clamp(v,a,b){ return Math.max(a, Math.min(b, Number(v))); }
14111:   function list(){ return Array.isArray(state.photos) ? state.photos : []; }
14112:   function esc(s){ return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"]/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;'}[m])); }
14113: 
14114:   function cssVar(name, fallback=''){
14115:     const v = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name);
14116:     return (v||'').trim() || fallback;
14117:   }
14118:   function hexToRgb(hex){
14119:     let h=String(hex||'').trim();
14120:     if(!h) return {r:124,g:92,b:255};
14121:     if(h.startsWith('rgb')){
14122:       const m=h.match(/rgba?\(([^)]+)\)/i);
14123:       if(m){ const parts=m[1].split(',').map(x=>parseFloat(x)); return {r:parts[0]||0,g:parts[1]||0,b:parts[2]||0}; }
14124:     }
14125:     if(h[0]==='#') h=h.slice(1);
14126:     if(h.length===3) h=h.split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
14127:     const n=parseInt(h,16);
14128:     return {r:(n>>16)&255,g:(n>>8)&255,b:n&255};
14129:   }
14130:   function rgba(c,a=1){ return `rgba(${c.r},${c.g},${c.b},${a})`; }
14131:   function mix(c1,c2,t){ return {r:Math.round(c1.r+(c2.r-c1.r)*t),g:Math.round(c1.g+(c2.g-c1.g)*t),b:Math.round(c1.b+(c2.b-c1.b)*t)}; }
14132:   function currentPalette(){
14133:     const c1 = hexToRgb(cssVar('--c1','#7c5cff'));
14134:     const c2 = hexToRgb(cssVar('--c2','#ff9fd6'));
14135:     const blendRaw = cssVar('--blend','50%');
14136:     const blend = clamp(parseFloat(String(blendRaw).replace('%',''))||50,0,100)/100;
14137:     return {c1,c2,blend};
14138:   }
14139:   function drawGradientBackground(ctx,w,h){
14140:     const {c1,c2,blend} = currentPalette();
14141:     ctx.save();
14142:     ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
14143:     const linear = ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,w,h);
14144:     linear.addColorStop(0, rgba(mix(c1,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.34),1));
14145:     linear.addColorStop(Math.max(0.08, Math.min(0.92, blend)), rgba(mix(c1,c2,0.5),1));
14146:     linear.addColorStop(1, rgba(mix(c2,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.14),1));
14147:     ctx.fillStyle = linear;
14148:     ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
14149: 
14150:     function orb(x,y,r,colA, alphaStart, alphaEnd){
14151:       const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);
14152:       g.addColorStop(0, rgba(colA,alphaStart));
14153:       g.addColorStop(1, rgba(colA,alphaEnd));
14154:       ctx.fillStyle=g; ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
14155:     }
14156:     orb(w*0.16,h*0.18,Math.max(w,h)*0.36,mix(c2,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.18),0.70,0);
14157:     orb(w*0.82,h*0.16,Math.max(w,h)*0.40,mix(c1,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.10),0.62,0);
14158:     orb(w*0.26,h*0.82,Math.max(w,h)*0.44,mix(c1,c2,0.28),0.38,0);
14159:     orb(w*0.84,h*0.84,Math.max(w,h)*0.42,mix(c2,c1,0.22),0.34,0);
14160: 
14161:     const sheen = ctx.createLinearGradient(0,h*0.04,w*0.96,h*0.82);
14162:     sheen.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.22)');
14163:     sheen.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(255,255,255,.08)');
15161:     // Prefer an on-demand stream (captureStream(0) + requestFrame): exactly one captured
15162:     // frame per rendered frame, so a slow async draw can't cause duplicated/dropped frames
15163:     // (a common source of stutter). Fall back to auto-sampling if unsupported.
15164:     let stream,track,manual=false;
15165:     try{
15166:       stream=canvas.captureStream(0); track=stream.getVideoTracks&&stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
15167:       if(track && typeof track.requestFrame==='function') manual=true; else stream=canvas.captureStream(fps);
15168:     }catch(e){ stream=canvas.captureStream(fps); }
15169:     const rec=new MediaRecorder(stream,{mimeType:mime}),parts=[]; rec.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size)parts.push(e.data);}; rec.start();
15170:     const frames=Math.max(2,Math.round(durationMs/1000*fps)), frameMs=1000/fps;
15171:     for(let f=0;f<frames;f++){
15172:       const t0=performance.now();
15173:       await drawFn(canvas,f/(frames-1));
15174:       if(manual && track) track.requestFrame();
15175:       // Keep real-time pacing (subtract draw time so the clip doesn't run long).
15176:       await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, Math.max(0, frameMs-(performance.now()-t0))));
15177:     }
15178:     await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,160));
15179:     return await new Promise(resolve=>{rec.onstop=()=>resolve({blob:new Blob(parts,{type:mime}),mime,name:'caseflow_landmark_morph'+(mime==='video/mp4'?'.mp4':'.webm')}); rec.stop();});
15180:   }
15181:   window.exportPreview=async function(){
15182:     if(list().length<2){toastFn('2枚以上の画像が必要です');return;}
15183:     toastFn('Landmark Morph 動画を書き出し中…');
15184:     const [w,h]=outputSize();
15185:     const result=await recordCanvas((canvas,t)=>drawSequence(canvas,t),w,h,Math.max(1200,Number(state.v75Settings.duration||3)*1000));
15186:     if(result && typeof setExportBlob==='function'){setExportBlob(result.blob,result.name,result.mime); toastFn('動画を書き出しました');}
15187:   };
15188:   window.exportMp4=window.exportPreview;
15189:   window.exportPairPreview=window.exportPreview;
15190: 
15191:   const prevGo=window.go;
15192:   if(typeof prevGo==='function'){
15193:     window.go=function(id){
15194:       prevGo(id);
15195:       if(id==='align') setTimeout(()=>renderAlign(),80);
15196:       if(id==='preview') setTimeout(()=>renderPreview(),80);
15197:     };
15198:   }

14114:   function cssVar(name, fallback=''){
14115:     const v = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue(name);
14116:     return (v||'').trim() || fallback;
14117:   }
14118:   function hexToRgb(hex){
14119:     let h=String(hex||'').trim();
14120:     if(!h) return {r:124,g:92,b:255};
14121:     if(h.startsWith('rgb')){
14122:       const m=h.match(/rgba?\(([^)]+)\)/i);
14123:       if(m){ const parts=m[1].split(',').map(x=>parseFloat(x)); return {r:parts[0]||0,g:parts[1]||0,b:parts[2]||0}; }
14124:     }
14125:     if(h[0]==='#') h=h.slice(1);
14126:     if(h.length===3) h=h.split('').map(c=>c+c).join('');
14127:     const n=parseInt(h,16);
14128:     return {r:(n>>16)&255,g:(n>>8)&255,b:n&255};
14129:   }
14130:   function rgba(c,a=1){ return `rgba(${c.r},${c.g},${c.b},${a})`; }
14131:   function mix(c1,c2,t){ return {r:Math.round(c1.r+(c2.r-c1.r)*t),g:Math.round(c1.g+(c2.g-c1.g)*t),b:Math.round(c1.b+(c2.b-c1.b)*t)}; }
14132:   function currentPalette(){
14133:     const c1 = hexToRgb(cssVar('--c1','#7c5cff'));
14134:     const c2 = hexToRgb(cssVar('--c2','#ff9fd6'));
14135:     const blendRaw = cssVar('--blend','50%');
14136:     const blend = clamp(parseFloat(String(blendRaw).replace('%',''))||50,0,100)/100;
14137:     return {c1,c2,blend};
14138:   }
14139:   function drawGradientBackground(ctx,w,h){
14140:     const {c1,c2,blend} = currentPalette();
14141:     ctx.save();
14142:     ctx.clearRect(0,0,w,h);
14143:     const linear = ctx.createLinearGradient(0,0,w,h);
14144:     linear.addColorStop(0, rgba(mix(c1,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.34),1));
14145:     linear.addColorStop(Math.max(0.08, Math.min(0.92, blend)), rgba(mix(c1,c2,0.5),1));
14146:     linear.addColorStop(1, rgba(mix(c2,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.14),1));
14147:     ctx.fillStyle = linear;
14148:     ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
14149: 
14150:     function orb(x,y,r,colA, alphaStart, alphaEnd){
14151:       const g=ctx.createRadialGradient(x,y,0,x,y,r);
14152:       g.addColorStop(0, rgba(colA,alphaStart));
14153:       g.addColorStop(1, rgba(colA,alphaEnd));
14154:       ctx.fillStyle=g; ctx.fillRect(0,0,w,h);
14155:     }
14156:     orb(w*0.16,h*0.18,Math.max(w,h)*0.36,mix(c2,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.18),0.70,0);
14157:     orb(w*0.82,h*0.16,Math.max(w,h)*0.40,mix(c1,{r:255,g:255,b:255},0.10),0.62,0);
14158:     orb(w*0.26,h*0.82,Math.max(w,h)*0.44,mix(c1,c2,0.28),0.38,0);
14159:     orb(w*0.84,h*0.84,Math.max(w,h)*0.42,mix(c2,c1,0.22),0.34,0);
14160: 
14161:     const sheen = ctx.createLinearGradient(0,h*0.04,w*0.96,h*0.82);
14162:     sheen.addColorStop(0,'rgba(255,255,255,.22)');
14163:     sheen.addColorStop(0.5,'rgba(255,255,255,.08)');
14164:     sheen.addColorStop(1,'rgba(255,255,255,0)');
14165:     ctx.fillStyle=sheen;
15163:     // (a common source of stutter). Fall back to auto-sampling if unsupported.
15164:     let stream,track,manual=false;
15165:     try{
15166:       stream=canvas.captureStream(0); track=stream.getVideoTracks&&stream.getVideoTracks()[0];
15167:       if(track && typeof track.requestFrame==='function') manual=true; else stream=canvas.captureStream(fps);
15168:     }catch(e){ stream=canvas.captureStream(fps); }
15169:     const rec=new MediaRecorder(stream,{mimeType:mime}),parts=[]; rec.ondataavailable=e=>{if(e.data&&e.data.size)parts.push(e.data);}; rec.start();
15170:     const frames=Math.max(2,Math.round(durationMs/1000*fps)), frameMs=1000/fps;
15171:     for(let f=0;f<frames;f++){
15172:       const t0=performance.now();
15173:       await drawFn(canvas,f/(frames-1));
15174:       if(manual && track) track.requestFrame();
15175:       // Keep real-time pacing (subtract draw time so the clip doesn't run long).
15176:       await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r, Math.max(0, frameMs-(performance.now()-t0))));
15177:     }
15178:     await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,160));
15179:     return await new Promise(resolve=>{rec.onstop=()=>resolve({blob:new Blob(parts,{type:mime}),mime,name:'caseflow_landmark_morph'+(mime==='video/mp4'?'.mp4':'.webm')}); rec.stop();});
15180:   }

20981: (function(){
20982:   if(window.__cfSwipeBack) return; window.__cfSwipeBack=true;
20983: 
20984:   // Lightweight navigation history so a back-swipe returns to the previous screen.
20985:   var navHistory=[], navigatingBack=false;
20986:   var prevGo=window.go;
20987:   window.go=function(id){
20988:     var cur=document.querySelector('.screen.active');
20989:     var curId=cur && cur.id;
20990:     var r = prevGo ? prevGo.apply(this,arguments) : undefined;
20991:     if(!navigatingBack && curId && curId!==id){
20992:       if(navHistory[navHistory.length-1]!==curId){
20993:         navHistory.push(curId);
20994:         if(navHistory.length>60) navHistory.shift();
20995:       }
20996:     }
20997:     return r;
20998:   };
20999:   window.cfGoBack=function(){
21000:     if(!navHistory.length) return false;
21001:     var target=navHistory.pop();
21002:     navigatingBack=true;
21003:     try{ window.go(target); } finally { navigatingBack=false; }
21004:     return true;
21005:   };
21006: 
21007:   // If an overlay / modal / sheet is open, let its own close control handle dismissal
21008:   // instead of navigating the screen underneath.
21009:   function overlayOpen(){
21010:     var sel='.cropModal,.photoManagerModal,.sampleVideoOverlay,.sampleVideoSheet,.studioChooserOverlay,.studioChooser,[role="dialog"]';
21011:     var els=document.querySelectorAll(sel);
21012:     for(var i=0;i<els.length;i++){
21013:       var el=els[i];
21014:       if(el.offsetParent===null) continue;            // not rendered
21015:       var cs=getComputedStyle(el);
21016:       if(cs.display!=='none' && cs.visibility!=='hidden' && parseFloat(cs.opacity||'1')>0.02 && el.offsetWidth>0 && el.offsetHeight>0){
21017:         return true;
21018:       }
21019:     }
21020:     return false;
21021:   }

21023:   // Edge-swipe (start near the left edge, drag right) — mirrors the iOS back gesture and
21024:   // avoids clashing with before/after compare sliders or horizontal content drags.
21025:   var sx=0, sy=0, st=0, tracking=false;
21026:   var EDGE=32, DIST_X=64, MAX_Y=80, MAX_T=800;
21027:   document.addEventListener('touchstart',function(e){
21028:     if(e.touches.length!==1){ tracking=false; return; }
21029:     var t=e.touches[0];
21030:     if(t.clientX<=EDGE && !overlayOpen()){ sx=t.clientX; sy=t.clientY; st=Date.now(); tracking=true; }
21031:     else tracking=false;
21032:   },{passive:true});
21033:   document.addEventListener('touchmove',function(e){
21034:     if(!tracking) return;
21035:     var t=e.touches[0]; if(!t) return;
21036:     // cancel if the gesture turns mostly vertical (it's a scroll, not a back-swipe)
21037:     if(Math.abs(t.clientY-sy) > Math.abs(t.clientX-sx) && Math.abs(t.clientY-sy)>24) tracking=false;
21038:   },{passive:true});
21039:   document.addEventListener('touchend',function(e){
21040:     if(!tracking) return; tracking=false;
21041:     var t=e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]; if(!t) return;
21042:     var dx=t.clientX-sx, dy=t.clientY-sy, dt=Date.now()-st;
21043:     if(dx>=DIST_X && Math.abs(dy)<=MAX_Y && dt<=MAX_T && !overlayOpen()){
21044:       window.cfGoBack();
21045:     }
21046:   },{passive:true});
21047: })();

21059:   // --- gray-world white balance (gentle, clamped so shade isn't wildly shifted) ---
21060:   function grayWorld(data){
21061:     var r=0,g=0,b=0,n=data.length/4,i;
21062:     for(i=0;i<data.length;i+=4){ r+=data[i]; g+=data[i+1]; b+=data[i+2]; }
21063:     r/=n; g/=n; b/=n; var gray=(r+g+b)/3;
21064:     var kr=gray/(r||1), kg=gray/(g||1), kb=gray/(b||1);
21065:     var cl=function(k){ return Math.min(1.5,Math.max(0.7,k)); };
21066:     kr=cl(kr); kg=cl(kg); kb=cl(kb);

21631: <script>
21632: /* Bulletproof Share-link handler.
21633:    Defined unconditionally here so it never depends on window.state being
21634:    ready (the earlier definition lived inside an IIFE that early-returns
21635:    when window.state is not yet set at parse time, leaving the Share button
21636:    wired to an undefined function). This standalone copy always wins. */
21637: window.cfOpenTopPage = function(){
21638:   var url = location.origin + location.pathname;
21639:   function toastMsg(m){ try{ if(typeof window.toast==='function'){ window.toast(m); return; } }catch(e){} }
21640:   function copyFallback(){
21641:     try{
21642:       var ta=document.createElement('textarea');
21643:       ta.value=url; ta.setAttribute('readonly','');
21644:       ta.style.cssText='position:fixed;top:-9999px;left:-9999px';
21645:       document.body.appendChild(ta);
21646:       ta.select(); ta.setSelectionRange(0, url.length);
21647:       var ok=false; try{ ok=document.execCommand('copy'); }catch(e){}
21648:       document.body.removeChild(ta);
21649:       if(ok){ toastMsg('リンクをコピーしました'); return; }
21650:     }catch(e){}
21651:     window.prompt('このリンクをコピーしてください', url);
21652:   }
21653:   if(navigator.share){
21654:     try{
21655:       navigator.share({title:'CASEFLOW STUDIO', text:'CASEFLOW STUDIO', url:url})
21656:         .catch(function(e){ if(e && e.name==='AbortError') return; copyFallback(); });
21657:       return;
21658:     }catch(e){ /* fall through to copy */ }
21659:   }
21660:   if(navigator.clipboard && navigator.clipboard.writeText){
21661:     navigator.clipboard.writeText(url).then(function(){ toastMsg('リンクをコピーしました'); }, copyFallback);
21662:     return;
21663:   }
21664:   copyFallback();
21665: };
21666: window.shareAppLink = window.cfOpenTopPage;

29162:   /* Background sync (does NOT require opening the Library screen): pull the
29163:      user's cloud items into the local store, then push any local-only items.
29164:      This is what makes "generate in Safari → see it in the PWA" work even if
29165:      the Library screen is never opened in the pushing context. */
29166:   var lastBgSync=0;
29167:   async function backgroundSync(reason){
29168:     var now=Date.now();

29171:     var sb=sbc();
29172:     if(!sb){ log('background sync skipped: no supabase client (',reason,')'); return; }
29173:     var user=await getUser();
29174:     if(!user){ log('background sync skipped: not signed in (',reason,')'); return; }
29175:     log('background sync start (',reason,')');
29176:     try{ await pullFromSupabase(); }catch(e){ log('bg pull failed', e&&e.message); }
29177:     scheduleReconcilePush();
29178:     /* refresh the list if the user is currently looking at it */
29179:     try{ var scr=document.getElementById('library'); if(scr&&scr.classList.contains('active')&&typeof window.renderLibrary==='function') window.renderLibrary(); }catch(e){}
29180:   }
